                                      Task Order No. 1

                  SCOPE OF WORK
                   EMILY DRIVE
     LOW PRESSURE SANITARY SEWER EXTENSION
          WATER AND SEWER DEPARTMENT
         CITY OF SEVIERVILLE, TENNESSEE


The purpose of this proposal is for the preliminary and final engineering design of approximately 1,100
linear feet of 2-inch diameter HDPE low pressure sanitary sewer (LPSS) (size to be confirmed during design)
to convey sanitary sewage from approximately fifteen (15) residential E/One pump stations and service
lines to the City’s public gravity sewer system. The receiving manhole is located near the intersection of
United Boulevard and the road serving the Catlettsburg Elementary School in Sevierville. Consor
(Consultant) proposes the following engineering services at the request of the City of Sevierville (Owner).

Scope of Services
Consultant will perform the following services.

Task 1 – Project Management
Consultant will provide overall leadership and team guidance aligned with Owner’s objectives. Coordinate,
monitor, and control the project resources to meet the technical, communications, and contractual
obligations required for developing and implementing the project scope.

Invoices/Status Reports
Consultant will prepare monthly invoices, including expenditures by task, hours worked by project
personnel, and other direct expenses with the associated backup documentation. Monthly status reports
will accompany each invoice and include work performed on the project during the previous month.

Coordination with Owner
Consultant will maintain communication with the Owner through virtual meetings and email.

Management and Coordination of Staff
Consultant will manage and coordinate the technical and scope issues of the overall project with staff.

Quality Assessment/Quality Control
Consultant will review internally the technical development, project risk, and deliverables and confirm the
scope of the project meets the quality standard of the Consultant.




                                                                                                          205
                                                                                                           1


Task Deliverables
     Monthly invoice and status report.

     Meeting summary notes for client meetings.

Assumptions
     Consultant assumes a Notice to Proceed date by November 1, 2026.
     Consultant assumes two (2) 30-minute virtual meetings will be held with the Consultant’s team and
      Owner’s Project Manager.
     Project duration (from NTP to final plans) will be approximately 3 months; therefore, it is assumed
      that there will be up to 3 invoices/status reports.


Task 2- Preliminary Design/Base Map Development
The Owner will acquire and provide data to the Consultant for the design using TN LIDAR data, GIS data,
and available imaging. A TN One Call and field visit may be performed to supplement the data. Any
available survey data provided by the Owner will be used to further assist in the design. Consultant will
utilize the provided base map drawing/information for the project design and development. The base
map drawing shall be provided in AutoCAD format. No additional survey will be provided by the
Consultant unless specifically requested by the Owner.
A virtual kick off meeting will be scheduled to discuss the Owner’s preferences, expectations, introduce the
design team, identify communications protocol, revisit proposed system layout, establish project schedule,
and other critical design criteria to be utilized.

Task Deliverables
     Consultant shall deliver to the Owner preliminary drawings in PDF format.

Assumptions
     Design will be based on using pre-engineered E/One residential grinder pumps and vaults.

     Location of the individual grinder pump stations will be shown at an assumed location by the
      Consultant using information provided by the Owner. Hydraulic calculations will be based on the
      worst-case scenario between the lowest property elevation and an approximate elevation 10 feet
      below the building’s finished floor elevation, if available. Actual location of the E/One pump station
      will be determined during the tank installation by the Owner.

     Location and demolition activities associated with the existing septic tanks, drain fields, and other
      utilities are not included in this scope of work.

     All utilities (below and above ground), driveways, and other physical objects along the pipeline
      route shall be identified, collected, and provided on the base map drawing by the Owner.

     The Owner shall provide access to approved standard specifications, approved construction details,
      and other pre-approved documents and information to be utilized for this project. The format of




                                                                                                        206
2


        approved specs shall be in Word. The construction details can be in CAD if changes/modifications
        are required or in PDF format if they are to be used as is.



Task 3 – Final Design
Consultant will develop a low pressure sanitary sewer design in coordination with the Owner’s
preferences and preliminary design efforts.
The design will begin at the intersection of Emily Drive and Douglas Dam Road and end at the connection
to the public gravity sewer system along United Boulevard and the road serving the Catlettsburg
Elementary School. Service line and associated utilities will be shown along the Right of the Way, while
the proposed low pressure sanitary sewer line will be in the road or in the right of way (to be decided by
the Owner). The required offset from any water lines will be maintained.
The engineering design and drawings will not cover the E/One pump station details and/or the exact
installation of the residential PS on each of the private residential lots. Owner will determine and locate
each residential unit during the construction phase of the project.
Consultant will submit 90 percent drawings and calculations to the Owner for their review and approval.
Consultant will address comments from Owner and update the drawings as part of the design task.

An in-person workshop will be conducted to review comments from the 90 percent submittal.

Deliverables
     Consultant will submit a design report in PDF format. Design report will include supporting material
      from the Owner and suppliers.

     Consultant will submit final drawings in PDF format.

Assumptions
     Consultant assumes the drawings will consist of the following sheets:

        1. Title sheet
        2. Legends/Symbols/Abbreviations
        3. List of drawings/General Notes
        4. Site layout sheet 1
        5. Site Layout Sheet 2
        6. Line Profile Sheet 1
        7. Line Profile Sheet 2
        8. Construction Details Sheet (utilizing Owner’s standard drawings as much as possible)
 Drawings will reference the Owner’s approved standard specifications, construction details, and
  manufacturer specific details.




                                                                                                          207
                                                                                                              3


 Consultant will develop a design report and perform low pressure sanitary sewer calculations as
  needed for permitting the project through TDEC. The LPSS line will be sized and based on typical
  domestic flow for the zoning of the lot(s), use of DH-71 or similar E/One grinder pumps and based on
  sufficient scouring velocities. Hydraulic design and pump selection will be confirmed with the
  manufacturer’s design guidance.

 Justification and confirmation that the existing gravity line has hydraulic capacity to receive the
  additional flow from this project shall be provided by the Owner.

 Consultant will generate a Hydraulic Grade Line Diagram for the worst-case pumping scenario.

 Owner will install the proposed low pressure sanitary sewer line and all residential E/One pump
  stations. Owner and resident will be responsible for making all utility connections to the E/One pump
  station to include connections to the main LPSS line, service line connections under the house, and
  electrical power service.


Task 4 – Permitting
Consultant will submit 100 percent drawings and design report to the Owner and TDEC for their review
and approval. Consultant will address comments from TDEC and update the drawings as needed as part
of the permitting task. Consultant will pay the permit fees on behalf of the Owner, and the fees will be
invoiced at actual cost.

Deliverables
     Consultant will submit final signed and sealed drawings and design report to TDEC for review and
      approval.

Assumptions
     No other permit preparation or submittal other than TDEC’s is included. TDEC submittal will include
      up to one revision or request for additional information.

Owner Responsibility
The Owner will provide the following information:

     Provide GIS, LiDAR, survey, and utility information.

     Obtain easements and site access and verify road ROW.

     Verify downstream gravity sewer capacity.

     Provide standard specifications and details.

     Determine final grinder pump locations.

     Install LPSS mains and pump stations.

     Obtain property access permissions.




                                                                                                        208
4


Exclusions
    No property or field survey is included in this proposal.

    No easement preparation is included in this proposal. If the design indicates that easements will
     be required for the work, additional survey and services will be covered by the Owner.

    The drawings will not include erosion control or traffic control sheets.

    No construction cost estimate will be provided or included with this task order.

    Front end bidding documents are not included in this task order.

    No bidding or construction management services are included in this task order. Such services can
     be provided if requested by the Owner.

    As-built drawings are not included at this time.

Schedule
Project duration is assumed to take 3 months from NTP to final signed and sealed drawings submitted to
TDEC for review and approval.

Budget
This agreement will be Lump Sum and billed in accordance with progress completed. Permit fee
reimbursement costs are not included in the total shown below. Anticipated Task budgets are listed below
with a man-hour estimate attached.

Specific Project Data
A. Title: Sevierville - Murphy Rd Ph 3 Gravity Sewer Replacement

B. Description: Provide professional and technical services for engineering design, permitting, and bidding services related to the
   replacement of approximately 1,650 linear feet of gravity sanitary sewer and 7 sanitary manholes. This will include design plans
   and specifications.

     Our Proposal was prepared based on the following assumptions:
         • If additional labor effort or change in schedule is required beyond described herein, OHM Advisors will negotiate an
             amendment with the City of Sevierville. OHM Advisors will not proceed with additional services without written
             authorization to proceed from the City of Sevierville.
         • The City of Sevierville will provide OHM with all information on hand related to the existing system.
         • Sewer modeling is not included in this proposal.
         • Task durations are estimated total times for each task through the duration of the entire project. The estimated days
             may not be consecutive.
         • Project will not require coordination for ARAP, SWPPP, Railroad, Communication Utilities, or others not explicitly
             listed in this proposal.
         • No wet weather crossings are anticipated or other efforts to temporarily maintain utility services of any kind.
         • Survey efforts will be provided for sewer design only and will not include easement preparation efforts.
                   o Easement descriptions would be additional services to those included in this project supplement.

1.   Services of CONSULTANT
     The CONSULTANT will provide professional services to the City of Sevierville for assistance in creating Cost Estimate,
     Engineering Plans, Specifications, Permitting, and Bid Documents:

     TASK 1: Project Startup / Project Management / Meetings
     • Initial review and coordination of Project Scope.
     • Preliminary Cost Estimate.
     • Project management duties, task coordination, schedule, and resource oversight.
     • Coordination with the City of Sevierville on existing conditions and proposed design.
     • Kick-off meeting and other coordination meetings, schedule for progress, and project close-out including:
              •    Prepare for and attend kick-off/overall project meeting.
              •    Coordination meetings as required.
              •    Prepare for and attend project wrap-up meeting.
              •    Assumed total of 6 meetings for project. Task 1 consisting of 1 meeting for design kickoff and schematic review.

     TASK 2: Engineering Plans and Specifications
     • OHM will prepare engineering plans for 8” SDR 26 PVC gravity sewer construction and sewer manhole replacement.

     • OHM will conduct Quality Assurance and Quality Control of the project documents.
             •   Assumed 1 meeting for Task 2.

     TASK 3: Permitting
     • OHM will complete and submit TDEC permits for approval.
     •  The client is responsible for permitting fees.
